Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive picture. Let me compile my findings:

## Extension Types/Categories Survey

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's what I found about how extension types are structured and where you'd need to make changes to add a new "cli" category:

### 1. **Enum Definition**
- **Location**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/types.ts` (line 5) and `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/db/schema/extension.ts` (lines 29–34)
- **Current values**: `"skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"`
- Both files define the enum; the schema version uses Drizzle's `pgEnum()` for the DB constraint.
- **Validator**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/validators/manifest.ts` (line 23) and `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/validators/filters.ts` (line 3)

### 2. **Database Schema**
- **Schema**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/db/schema/extension.ts` (lines 95–197)
  - `category` is a NOT NULL column using `extensionCategoryEnum()`, indexed at line 172
  - **Approvals table** (`approval.ts`, lines 38–107): `approval_requests` table stores `extensionCategory` as a snapshot at submission time (line 48), keyed in the reviewer queue index (line 82)
  - `approval_admins` table (lines 126–195) has `extensionCategory` as part of the 5-dim cell key; it participates in every routing fan-out and uniqueness constraint

### 3. **Where Category Is Branched On**
- **CLI installers**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/cli/src/installers/index.ts` (lines 8–25) — switch statement on category, currently only handles `skills` and `plugins` fall through to skill install. **Adding `cli` would need its own branch here.**
- **Manifest defaults**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/utils/publish.ts` (lines 22–34) — `defaultClassification()` function maps category to funcCat/subCat; `mcp` → "tools/integrations", `slash` → "tools/commands", default → "tools/general"
- **UI rendering**: 
  - `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/extension/ExtensionListing.vue` (line ~32) — conditionally renders MCP panorama link for `category === "mcp"`
  - `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/publish/WizardBasics.vue` — category radio buttons rendered from i18n labels
- **Routing**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/middleware/legacy-categories.global.ts` — redirects `?category=slash` → `/commands`; others map directly

### 4. **i18n Labels**
- **Location**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/i18n/locales/en.json` (search for `"publish.options.category"`)
- Current labels:
  ```json
  "options": {
    "category": {
      "skills": "Skills",
      "mcp": "MCP",
      "slash": "Slash command",
      "plugins": "Plugin"
    }
  }
  ```
- Same structure in `/i18n/locales/zh.json`

### 5. **Manifest Spec**
- **Location**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/docs/manifest-spec.md` (lines 48–80)
- Already category-aware: each category declares its own `[install.<agent>]` section with category-specific destination paths:
  - Skills: `skills = "~/.claude/skills/{slug}"`
  - MCP: `mcpConfig` + `mcpKey`
  - Slash: `commands = "~/.claude/commands/{slug}.md"`
  - Plugins: `plugins = "~/.claude/plugins/{slug}"`
- **Easily extensible**: add new section like `[install.claude]` with `cli = "~/.claude/cli/{slug}"` or similar

### 6. **Approvals Matrix**
- **Location**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/data/approval-admins.ts` (lines 1–91)
- The approval matrix is **already category-aware** — every extensionCategory tab has seed rows covering "All" tier (lines 41–44) plus macro/micro granularity
- **Schema**: `approval_admins` table (lines 126–195 in `approval.ts`) has `extensionCategory` as the first column in the 5-dim key; adding `cli` would just mean seed rows for `extensionCategory="cli"` with appropriate tier/categoryLevel combos
- **No migration required** — the enum just expands; existing seed structure scales

### 7. **Install Flow**
- **CLI**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/cli/src/commands/install.ts` (lines 1–66)
  - Fetches extension metadata (including category), downloads bundle, calls `installExtension(slug, ext.category, zipBuffer)`
  - **Category is passed through but barely used** — currently just calls skill installer for everything
  - **To support `cli`**: modify `installers/index.ts` to branch on the category and route to a CLI-specific installer that reads `[install.claude]` / `cli` from the manifest and installs accordingly

### Summary: Adding a "cli" Type

**Minimal changes needed** (8 places):
1. Enum definition (2 files) — add `"cli"` to type union
2. Validator — add to `CATEGORIES` array
3. Manifest spec — document `[install.claude] cli = "..."` section
4. CLI installer — add `case "cli":` branch with category-aware install logic
5. i18n labels (2 files) — add `"cli": "CLI"` to `publish.options.category`
6. Seed approval admins (optional) — add rows for `extensionCategory: "cli"` with covering cells
7. Default classification (optional) — decide funcCat/subCat for `cli` → likely `tools/cli` or similar
8. UI redirection (optional) — add route mapping if you want `/cli` instead of `/extensions?category=cli`

The **approval matrix and DB schema already scale**; no migration required. The **hardest part** is the CLI installer logic — parsing the manifest's `[install.cli]` section and actually installing files in the right places per agent conventions.
```

> AGENT

My take: the codebase scales cleanly to a new type — the enum + approvals matrix + manifest spec are all already category-keyed, so adding `cli` is mostly seed rows, validators, i18n, and an installer branch. The real question isn't *can* we add it but *what is it semantically*: a "CLI extension" risks overlapping with `plugins` (which can already ship scripts/binaries) and with the AgentCenter CLI itself, so the install destination, the manifest shape, and how it differs from `plugins` need to be nailed down before we touch the enum — once it's in `/api/v1`, the contract is frozen.

Let me ask a few clarifying questions before I draft a plan.

81	// Product-line lookup table — bilingual labels, kebab-case ids

83	// Used as the third axis of the reviewer matrix for the productLine tier,
84	// and as the company-level "endorsing line" on extensions whose
85	// officialTier='productLine'. Editable as data, not code, so adding a line
86	// is a content migration rather than a release.
87	export const productLines = pgTable("product_lines", {
88	  id: text().primaryKey(),
89	  labelEn: text().notNull(),
90	  labelZh: text().notNull(),
91	  sortOrder: integer().notNull().default(0),

95	export const extensions = pgTable(
96	  "extensions",
97	  {
98	    id: text().primaryKey(),
99	    slug: text().notNull().unique(),
100	    category: extensionCategoryEnum().notNull(),
101	    badge: extensionBadgeEnum(),
102	    scope: extensionScopeEnum().notNull(),
103	    // Official-tier approval outcome. Null = unofficial. See approval.ts.
104	    officialTier: officialTierEnum(),
105	    // Endorsing product line — required iff officialTier='productLine',
106	    // null otherwise. CHECK constraint below enforces the shape; the
107	    // approval orchestrator is the only writer. ON DELETE RESTRICT matches
108	    // the iff-rule: SET NULL would still fail the CHECK for every
109	    // productLine-tier row, so RESTRICT just surfaces the real outcome.
110	    productLineId: text().references(() => productLines.id, {
111	      onDelete: "restrict",
112	    }),
113	    // funcCat/subCat are nullable — the redesigned publish wizard does not
114	    // collect them; admin curation can backfill or system defaults apply.
115	    funcCat: funcCatEnum(),
116	    subCat: text(),
117	    l2: text(),
118	    publisherUserId: text().references(() => users.id, {
119	      onDelete: "set null",
120	    }),
121	    // Tier revocation audit trail. Populated when a super-admin demotes
122	    // an Official extension back to Unofficial via the detail page
123	    // Revoke action. Cleared on the next approval — see the orchestrator
124	    // in server/utils/approvals.ts. ON DELETE SET NULL on the admin FK
125	    // so deleting an admin orphans the attribution rather than failing
126	    // the cascade.
127	    revokedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
128	    revokedByUserId: text().references(() => users.id, {
129	      onDelete: "set null",
130	    }),
131	    revocationNote: text(),
132	    ownerOrgId: text()
133	      .notNull()
134	      .references(() => organizations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
135	    deptId: text().references(() => departments.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
136	    iconEmoji: text(),
137	    iconColor: text(),
138	    visibility: visibilityEnum().notNull().default("draft"),
139	    // Editorial flag — hand-curated. Surfaces a single extension on the home

141	    // card; `featured` is a transient "pick of the week" pointer.
142	    featured: boolean().notNull().default(false),

175	    index("idx_ext_product_line").on(t.productLineId),

1	import { z } from "zod";
2	
3	// Slug shape, exported so client-side form validators don't redefine it.
4	export const SLUG_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;
5	export const SEMVER_PATTERN = /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/;
6	
7	// "summary" is the redesigned wizard's one-line description; it lands in
8	// the existing `tagline` column. Capped at 80 chars to match the design.
9	export const SUMMARY_MAX = 80;
10	
11	// Single source of truth for what makes an extension manifest valid.
12	// Mirrors `docs/manifest-spec.md`. Bundle and form schemas below derive
13	// from this core; nothing should redefine these constraints elsewhere.
14	export const ExtensionManifestCore = z.object({
15	  slug: z
16	    .string()
17	    .min(3)
18	    .max(64)
19	    .regex(SLUG_PATTERN, "Lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens only"),
20	  name: z.string().min(2).max(80),
21	  nameZh: z.string().max(80).optional(),
22	  version: z.string().regex(SEMVER_PATTERN, "Must be semver (e.g. 1.0.0)"),
23	  category: z.enum(["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"]),
24	  scope: z.enum(["personal", "org", "enterprise"]),
25	  funcCat: z.enum(["workTask", "business", "tools"]),
26	  subCat: z.string().min(1).max(60),
27	  l2: z.string().max(60).optional(),
28	  description: z.string().min(1).max(280),
29	  descriptionZh: z.string().max(280).optional(),
30	  tagline: z.string().max(120).optional(),
31	});
32	
33	// Permissions toggled in the wizard — surfaced on the detail page so users
34	// know what the extension intends to do before installing.
35	export const PermissionsSchema = z
36	  .object({
37	    network: z.boolean().optional(),
38	    files: z.boolean().optional(),
39	    runtime: z.boolean().optional(),
40	    data: z.boolean().optional(),
41	  })
42	  .default({});
43	
44	// Form: the slimmer field set the redesigned publish wizard collects.
45	// Notable departures from the prior form:
46	//   - `summary` (the design's one-line description, max 80) replaces the
47	//     long `description`/`descriptionZh` pair. Stored in the `tagline` column.
48	//   - `funcCat` / `subCat` / `l2` are gone from the wizard. Server-side
49	//     defaults (`defaultClassification`) backfill funcCat/subCat from the
50	//     chosen `category` so filters keep working.
51	//   - URL/license fields (homepageUrl, repoUrl, licenseSpdx) are dropped
52	//     from the wizard; the columns stay nullable in the DB.
53	//   - `iconColor`, `readmeMd`, `permissions`, `sourceMethod`, `taglineZh`
54	//     are new in the form and persist directly.
55	export const ManifestFormSchema = z.object({
56	  slug: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.slug,
57	  name: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.name,
58	  nameZh: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.nameZh,
59	  version: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.version,
60	  category: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.category,
61	  scope: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.scope,
62	  summary: z.string().min(1, "Required").max(SUMMARY_MAX),
63	  taglineZh: z.string().max(SUMMARY_MAX).optional(),
64	  // Bound the README so saveDraft + step transitions don't ship
65	  // arbitrarily large markdown payloads on every keystroke. 16k is
66	  // generous for an extension README without ballooning auto-save.
67	  readmeMd: z.string().max(16_000).optional(),
68	  iconColor: z
69	    .enum(["indigo", "amber", "emerald", "rose", "slate"])
70	    .default("indigo"),
71	  tagIds: z.array(z.string()).max(8),
72	  deptId: z.string().optional(),
73	  permissions: PermissionsSchema,
74	  sourceMethod: z.literal("zip").default("zip"),
75	});
76	
77	export type ManifestFormValues = z.infer<typeof ManifestFormSchema>;
78	
79	// Bundle: same fields as before, lifted into the [extension] /
80	// [categorization] TOML sections defined in docs/manifest-spec.md. The
81	// bundle manifest still carries description + funcCat + subCat — those
82	// come from whatever produced the bundle (CLI, hand-authored TOML), not
83	// from the publish wizard, so they remain required at the bundle layer.
84	export const BundleManifestSchema = z.object({
85	  extension: ExtensionManifestCore.pick({
86	    slug: true,
87	    name: true,
88	    nameZh: true,
89	    version: true,
90	    category: true,
91	    scope: true,
92	    description: true,
93	    descriptionZh: true,
94	    tagline: true,
95	  }),
96	  categorization: ExtensionManifestCore.pick({
97	    funcCat: true,
98	    subCat: true,
99	    l2: true,
100	  }),
101	});
102	
103	export type BundleManifest = z.infer<typeof BundleManifestSchema>;
104

1	import { z } from "zod";
2	
3	const CATEGORIES = ["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"] as const;
4	const SCOPES = ["personal", "org", "enterprise"] as const;
5	const FUNC_CATS = ["workTask", "business", "tools"] as const;
6	const FILTER_CHIPS = ["all", "trending", "new", "official", "free"] as const;
7	const TIERS = ["unofficial", "productLine", "company"] as const;
8	const SORTS = ["downloads", "stars", "recent"] as const;
9	const TAG_MATCHES = ["any", "all"] as const;
10	
11	export const PAGE_SIZE = 24;
12	
13	export const filtersSchema = z.object({
14	  q: z.string().trim().min(1).max(120).optional(),
15	  category: z.enum(CATEGORIES).optional(),
16	  scope: z.enum(SCOPES).optional(),
17	  funcCat: z.enum(FUNC_CATS).optional(),
18	  subCat: z.string().trim().min(1).max(40).optional(),
19	  l2: z.string().trim().min(1).max(40).optional(),
20	  // Department id (dotted-path) or the literal "__all" to disable the filter.
21	  dept: z.string().trim().min(1).max(120).optional(),
22	  // Creator = users.id (the user who published). Schema column is named
23	  // publisherUserId for legacy reasons; we expose it as "creator" everywhere.
24	  creator: z.string().trim().min(1).max(80).optional(),
25	  // Publisher = organizations.id (the owning org).
26	  publisher: z.string().trim().min(1).max(80).optional(),
27	  tags: z.array(z.string().trim().min(1).max(40)).max(16).optional(),
28	  tagMatch: z.enum(TAG_MATCHES).optional(),
29	  filter: z.enum(FILTER_CHIPS).optional(),
30	  // Three-state official-tier filter: 'unofficial' | 'productLine' | 'company'.
31	  // Separate from the legacy `filter=official` chip (which now means "any
32	  // official tier"); `tier=` picks a specific cell.
33	  tier: z.enum(TIERS).optional(),
34	  // Optional product-line id (kebab-case). Only meaningful when paired with
35	  // `tier=productLine`; the listing query still honours it standalone (acts
36	  // as "narrow to this line") which keeps the filter rail simple.
37	  productLineId: z
38	    .string()
39	    .trim()
40	    .min(1)
41	    .max(40)
42	    .regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/)
43	    .optional(),
44	  sort: z.enum(SORTS).optional(),
45	  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(1000).optional(),
46	});
47	
48	export type Filters = z.infer<typeof filtersSchema>;
49	
50	/**
51	 * Parse Next.js searchParams into typed Filters. Invalid input is dropped,
52	 * so a malformed URL silently falls back to "no filter" rather than 500ing.
53	 */
54	export function parseFilters(
55	  input: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>,
56	): Filters {
57	  const tags = input.tags;
58	  const normalized = {
59	    ...input,
60	    tags:
61	      tags === undefined
62	        ? undefined
63	        : Array.isArray(tags)
64	          ? tags
65	          : tags.split(",").filter(Boolean),
66	  };
67	  const parsed = filtersSchema.safeParse(normalized);
68	  return parsed.success ? parsed.data : {};
69	}
70	
71	export function pageOffset(page: number | undefined) {
72	  return ((page ?? 1) - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
73	}
74	
75	/**
76	 * Adapter from `URLSearchParams` (the runtime shape) to the `Record` shape
77	 * `parseFilters` consumes. Repeated keys (`?tags=a&tags=b`) accumulate into
78	 * an array; `serializeFilters` always emits comma-joined, but external
79	 * links may use the array form and the validator already handles both.
80	 */
81	export function searchParamsToInput(
82	  searchParams: URLSearchParams,
83	): Record<string, string | string[]> {
84	  const out: Record<string, string | string[]> = {};
85	  for (const [key, value] of searchParams.entries()) {
86	    const prev = out[key];
87	    if (prev === undefined) {
88	      out[key] = value;
89	    } else if (Array.isArray(prev)) {
90	      prev.push(value);
91	    } else {
92	      out[key] = [prev, value];
93	    }
94	  }
95	  return out;
96	}
97	
98	/**
99	 * Inverse of `parseFilters`. Encodes typed filters back into URL search
100	 * params using the same conventions parseFilters consumes — comma-joined
101	 * arrays for `tags`, omitted keys for undefined / empty values.
102	 */
103	export function serializeFilters(filters: Partial<Filters>): URLSearchParams {
104	  const params = new URLSearchParams();
105	  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(filters)) {
106	    if (value === undefined || value === "" || value === null) continue;
107	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
108	      if (value.length > 0) params.set(key, value.join(","));
109	    } else {
110	      params.set(key, String(value));
111	    }
112	  }
113	  return params;
114	}
115

1	import { installSkill, uninstallSkill, listInstalledSlugs } from "./skills";
2	
3	export async function installExtension(
4	  slug: string,
5	  category: string,
6	  zipBuffer: ArrayBuffer,
7	): Promise<string> {
8	  switch (category) {
9	    case "skill":
10	    case "skills":
11	      return installSkill(slug, zipBuffer);
12	    default:
13	      // Fallback: treat everything else as a skill-style directory drop
14	      return installSkill(slug, zipBuffer);
15	  }
16	}
17	
18	export async function uninstallExtension(slug: string, category: string): Promise<boolean> {
19	  switch (category) {
20	    case "skill":
21	    case "skills":
22	      return uninstallSkill(slug);
23	    default:
24	      return uninstallSkill(slug);
25	  }
26	}
27	
28	export { listInstalledSlugs };

1	// Dev-seed admin matrix for the redesigned approval workflow. Each row
2	// maps a 5-coord cell to one admin email:
3	//   (extensionCategory × tier × productLineId? × categoryLevel × categoryKey)
4	//
5	// The DB CHECKs enforce both shape rules — productLineId iff
6	// tier='productLine', and categoryKey='*' iff categoryLevel='all'. The
7	// seed script mirrors both here so a bad row in this file fails loudly
8	// instead of at INSERT time.
9	//
10	// Emails resolve against `CREATORS` in `scripts/seed.ts`. Real prod
11	// assignment happens through the matrix UI.
12	//
13	// Coverage strategy: every ext-type tab has at least one cell with a
14	// visible admin so the unified table doesn't render an empty
15	// matrix on a fresh seed. Skills is the demo tab and gets the densest
16	// coverage (All + every macro + a few PL + a micro example); the other
17	// three are sparser. The four seeded `approval_requests` rows in
18	// `approval-requests.ts` each have at least one covering admin so the
19	// `/admin/approvals` queue lights up under multiple personas.
20	
21	export type SeedProductLine = "wireless" | "datacom" | "terminals" | "cloud"
22	export type SeedExtensionCategory = "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"
23	export type SeedAdminCategoryLevel = "all" | "macro" | "micro"
24	
25	export interface SeedApprovalAdmin {
26	  extensionCategory: SeedExtensionCategory
27	  tier: "productLine" | "company"
28	  productLineId?: SeedProductLine
29	  categoryLevel: SeedAdminCategoryLevel
30	  // '*' when categoryLevel='all'; a FUNC_TAXONOMY l1 key for 'macro';
31	  // an l2 key for 'micro'.
32	  categoryKey: string
33	  reviewerEmail: string
34	}
35	
36	export const APPROVAL_ADMINS: ReadonlyArray<SeedApprovalAdmin> = [
37	  // --- Universal All admins (one per ext-type) ---
38	  // Amy holds (anyExtType, company, All) — the global fallback so every
39	  // company-tier request has a reviewer even before category-specific
40	  // admins are seeded.
41	  { extensionCategory: "skills",  tier: "company", categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*", reviewerEmail: "amy@agentcenter.dev" },
42	  { extensionCategory: "mcp",     tier: "company", categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*", reviewerEmail: "amy@agentcenter.dev" },
43	  { extensionCategory: "slash",   tier: "company", categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*", reviewerEmail: "amy@agentcenter.dev" },
44	  { extensionCategory: "plugins", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*", reviewerEmail: "amy@agentcenter.dev" },
45	
46	  // --- Skills tab: every macro covered ---
47	  // Three people split the nine macros along funcCat lines.
48	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "systemDesign", reviewerEmail: "ben@agentcenter.dev" },
49	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "softDev",      reviewerEmail: "ben@agentcenter.dev" },
50	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "testing",      reviewerEmail: "ben@agentcenter.dev" },
51	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "network",      reviewerEmail: "cory@agentcenter.dev" },
52	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "embedded",     reviewerEmail: "cory@agentcenter.dev" },
53	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "cloud",        reviewerEmail: "cory@agentcenter.dev" },
54	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "docs",         reviewerEmail: "eli@agentcenter.dev" },
55	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "data",         reviewerEmail: "eli@agentcenter.dev" },
56	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "company", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "vcs",          reviewerEmail: "fei@agentcenter.dev" },
57	
58	  // --- Skills tab: per-product-line All admins ---
59	  // Routing requires exact tier+PL match, so a company-tier admin can't
60	  // pick up a PL request. These ensure every Skills × PL request has at
61	  // least one queue subscriber.
62	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "wireless",  categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*", reviewerEmail: "dao@agentcenter.dev" },
63	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "datacom",   categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*", reviewerEmail: "fei@agentcenter.dev" },
64	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "terminals", categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*", reviewerEmail: "eli@agentcenter.dev" },
65	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "cloud",     categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*", reviewerEmail: "ben@agentcenter.dev" },
66	
67	  // --- Skills tab: a couple of macro × PL refinements + one micro ---
68	  // These coexist with the All row above — a publisher request matches
69	  // every covering admin, so dedup happens in SQL (SELECT DISTINCT).
70	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "wireless", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "softDev",  reviewerEmail: "ben@agentcenter.dev" },
71	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "wireless", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "network",  reviewerEmail: "cory@agentcenter.dev" },
72	  { extensionCategory: "skills", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "wireless", categoryLevel: "micro", categoryKey: "backend",  reviewerEmail: "ben@agentcenter.dev" },
73	
74	  // --- MCP tab: a couple of macros + one PL All ---
75	  { extensionCategory: "mcp", tier: "company",     categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "softDev", reviewerEmail: "ben@agentcenter.dev" },
76	  { extensionCategory: "mcp", tier: "company",     categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "network", reviewerEmail: "cory@agentcenter.dev" },
77	  { extensionCategory: "mcp", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "wireless", categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*", reviewerEmail: "dao@agentcenter.dev" },
78	
79	  // --- Slash tab: macros covering the seeded pending /translate request ---
80	  // /translate is owned by ben at subCat=docs; the e2e suite exercises
81	  // (slash, productLine, wireless) so Eli needs to cover that exact
82	  // column-tier shape. Without this row the e2e queue would be empty.
83	  { extensionCategory: "slash", tier: "company",     categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "docs", reviewerEmail: "fei@agentcenter.dev" },
84	  { extensionCategory: "slash", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "wireless", categoryLevel: "macro", categoryKey: "docs", reviewerEmail: "eli@agentcenter.dev" },
85	
86	  // --- Plugins tab: cover the seeded pending mqtt-bridge request ---
87	  // mqtt-bridge is plugins × productLine wireless × network. Without a
88	  // matching admin the PL queue is empty for everyone but super-admins.
89	  { extensionCategory: "plugins", tier: "productLine", productLineId: "wireless", categoryLevel: "all", categoryKey: "*",      reviewerEmail: "dao@agentcenter.dev" },
90	  { extensionCategory: "plugins", tier: "company",     categoryLevel: "macro",    categoryKey: "data", reviewerEmail: "eli@agentcenter.dev" },
91	]
92	
93	// First entry in `CREATORS` (amy) gets the super-admin membership unless
94	// `SEED_SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL` overrides at runtime.
95	export const DEFAULT_SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL = "amy@agentcenter.dev"

1	const CATEGORY_REDIRECTS: Record<string, string> = {
2	  skills: "/skills",
3	  mcp: "/mcp",
4	  slash: "/commands",
5	  plugins: "/plugins",
6	}
7	
8	export default defineNuxtRouteMiddleware((to) => {
9	  if (to.path.endsWith("/mcp-panorama")) {
10	    return navigateTo(
11	      {
12	        path: to.path.replace(/\/mcp-panorama$/, "/mcp/panorama"),
13	        query: to.query,
14	      },
15	      { replace: true },
16	    )
17	  }
18	
19	  if (to.path.endsWith("/extensions") && typeof to.query.category === "string") {
20	    const dest = CATEGORY_REDIRECTS[to.query.category]
21	    if (dest) {
22	      const { category: _category, ...rest } = to.query
23	      return navigateTo(
24	        { path: to.path.replace(/\/extensions$/, dest), query: rest },
25	        { replace: true },
26	      )
27	    }
28	  }
29	})
30

13	} from "~~/shared/validators/manifest"
14	import { submit, VersionStateError } from "~~/server/utils/extensions-state"
15	
16	const DEFAULT_ORG_ID = "default"
17	
18	function emptyToNull(v: string | undefined | null): string | null {
19	  return v == null || v === "" ? null : v
20	}
21	
22	function defaultClassification(category: ManifestFormValues["category"]): {
23	  funcCat: "tools" | "workTask" | "business"
24	  subCat: string
25	} {
26	  switch (category) {
27	    case "mcp":
28	      return { funcCat: "tools", subCat: "integrations" }
29	    case "slash":
30	      return { funcCat: "tools", subCat: "commands" }
31	    default:
32	      return { funcCat: "tools", subCat: "general" }
33	  }
34	}
35	
36	export type CreateDraftResult =
37	  | { ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }
38	  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string }
39	
40	export async function createDraftExtension(
41	  userId: string,
42	  raw: ManifestFormValues,
43	): Promise<CreateDraftResult> {
44	  const parsed = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(raw)
45	  if (!parsed.success) {
46	    return {
47	      ok: false,
48	      error: "invalid_input",
49	      detail: parsed.error.issues.map((i) => i.message).join("; "),
50	    }
51	  }
52	  const data = parsed.data
53	  const cls = defaultClassification(data.category)
54	  const extensionId = crypto.randomUUID()
55	  const versionId = crypto.randomUUID()
56	  const db = useDb()
57	
58	  try {
59	    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
60	      await tx.insert(extensions).values({
61	        id: extensionId,
62	        slug: data.slug,
63	        name: data.name,
64	        nameZh: emptyToNull(data.nameZh),
65	        tagline: data.summary,
66	        taglineZh: emptyToNull(data.taglineZh),
67	        category: data.category,
68	        scope: data.scope,
69	        funcCat: cls.funcCat,
70	        subCat: cls.subCat,
71	        deptId: emptyToNull(data.deptId),
72	        iconColor: data.iconColor,
73	        readmeMd: emptyToNull(data.readmeMd ?? ""),
74	        permissions: data.permissions ?? {},
75	        publisherUserId: userId,
76	        ownerOrgId: DEFAULT_ORG_ID,
77	        visibility: "draft",
78	      })
79	      await tx.insert(extensionVersions).values({
80	        id: versionId,
81	        extensionId,
82	        version: data.version,
83	        status: "pending",
84	        sourceMethod: data.sourceMethod,
85	      })
86	      if (data.tagIds.length > 0) {
87	        await tx.insert(extensionTags).values(
88	          data.tagIds.map((tagId) => ({ extensionId, tagId })),
89	        )
90	      }
91	    })
92	  } catch (err) {
93	    console.error("[publish] createDraftExtension failed", err)
94	    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
95	    if (message.includes("extensions_slug_unique") || message.includes("duplicate key")) {
96	      return { ok: false, error: "slug_taken" }
97	    }
98	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error", detail: message }
99	  }
100	  return { ok: true, extensionId, versionId }
101	}
102	
103	export type UpdateDraftResult =
104	  | { ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }
105	  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string }
106	
107	/**
108	 * Update an existing draft extension. Slug and version are immutable once
109	 * the draft is created (the upload pipeline keys off them), so this only
110	 * touches the mutable wizard fields. tagIds are diff-replaced.
111	 */
112	export async function updateDraftExtension(
113	  userId: string,
114	  extensionId: string,
115	  raw: ManifestFormValues,
116	): Promise<UpdateDraftResult> {
117	  const parsed = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(raw)
118	  if (!parsed.success) {
119	    return {
120	      ok: false,
121	      error: "invalid_input",
122	      detail: parsed.error.issues.map((i) => i.message).join("; "),
123	    }
124	  }
125	  const data = parsed.data
126	  const cls = defaultClassification(data.category)
127	  const db = useDb()
128	
129	  const [existing] = await db
130	    .select({
131	      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,
132	      visibility: extensions.visibility,
133	      slug: extensions.slug,
134	    })
135	    .from(extensions)
136	    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))
137	    .limit(1)
138	  if (!existing || existing.publisherUserId !== userId) {
139	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" }
140	  }
141	  if (existing.visibility !== "draft") {
142	    return { ok: false, error: "not_editable" }
143	  }
144	  if (existing.slug !== data.slug) {
145	    return { ok: false, error: "slug_immutable" }
146	  }
147	
148	  const [latestVersion] = await db
149	    .select({ id: extensionVersions.id, version: extensionVersions.version })
150	    .from(extensionVersions)
151	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensionId))
152	    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))
153	    .limit(1)
154	  if (!latestVersion) return { ok: false, error: "not_found" }
155	  if (latestVersion.version !== data.version) {
156	    return { ok: false, error: "version_immutable" }
157	  }
158	
159	  try {
160	    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
161	      await tx
162	        .update(extensions)
163	        .set({
164	          name: data.name,
165	          nameZh: emptyToNull(data.nameZh),
166	          tagline: data.summary,
167	          taglineZh: emptyToNull(data.taglineZh),
168	          category: data.category,
169	          funcCat: cls.funcCat,
170	          subCat: cls.subCat,
171	          scope: data.scope,
172	          deptId: emptyToNull(data.deptId),
173	          iconColor: data.iconColor,
174	          readmeMd: emptyToNull(data.readmeMd ?? ""),
175	          permissions: data.permissions ?? {},
176	        })
177	        .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))
178	      await tx
179	        .delete(extensionTags)
180	        .where(eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensionId))
181	      if (data.tagIds.length > 0) {
182	        await tx.insert(extensionTags).values(
183	          data.tagIds.map((tagId) => ({ extensionId, tagId })),
184	        )
185	      }
186	    })
187	  } catch (err) {
188	    console.error("[publish] updateDraftExtension failed", err)
189	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error", detail: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) }
190	  }
191	  return { ok: true, extensionId, versionId: latestVersion.id }
192	}
193	
194	export type GetDraftResult =
195	  | { ok: true; draft: DraftRehydration }
196	  | { ok: false; error: "not_found" | "not_editable" }
197	
198	export interface DraftRehydration {
199	  extensionId: string
200	  versionId: string
201	  bundleUploaded: boolean
202	  formValues: ManifestFormValues
203	}
204	
205	/**
206	 * Rehydrate a draft for the resume/edit flow. Owner-checked; only drafts
207	 * (not published/scanning/etc.) are returned. The form values come from
208	 * the same shape ManifestFormSchema produces so the wizard can drop them
209	 * in without remapping.
210	 */
211	export async function getDraftExtension(
212	  userId: string,
213	  extensionId: string,
214	): Promise<GetDraftResult> {
215	  const db = useDb()
216	  const [ext] = await db
217	    .select()
218	    .from(extensions)
219	    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))
220	    .limit(1)
221	  if (!ext || ext.publisherUserId !== userId) {
222	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" }
223	  }
224	  if (ext.visibility !== "draft") {
225	    return { ok: false, error: "not_editable" }
226	  }
227	
228	  const [version] = await db
229	    .select()
230	    .from(extensionVersions)
231	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensionId))
232	    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))
233	    .limit(1)
234	  if (!version) return { ok: false, error: "not_found" }
235	
236	  const tagRows = await db
237	    .select({ tagId: extensionTags.tagId })
238	    .from(extensionTags)
239	    .where(eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensionId))
240	
241	  const formValues: ManifestFormValues = {
242	    slug: ext.slug,
243	    name: ext.name,
244	    nameZh: ext.nameZh ?? undefined,
245	    version: version.version,
246	    category: ext.category,
247	    scope: ext.scope,
248	    summary: ext.tagline ?? "",
249	    taglineZh: ext.taglineZh ?? undefined,
250	    readmeMd: ext.readmeMd ?? undefined,
251	    iconColor: (ext.iconColor as ManifestFormValues["iconColor"]) ?? "indigo",
252	    tagIds: tagRows.map((r) => r.tagId),
253	    deptId: ext.deptId ?? undefined,
254	    permissions: (ext.permissions ?? {}) as ManifestFormValues["permissions"],
255	    sourceMethod: (version.sourceMethod as ManifestFormValues["sourceMethod"]) ?? "zip",
256	  }
257	
258	  return {
259	    ok: true,
260	    draft: {
261	      extensionId: ext.id,
262	      versionId: version.id,
263	      bundleUploaded: Boolean(version.bundleFileId),
264	      formValues,
265	    },
266	  }
267	}
268	
269	export type AttachFileResult =
270	  | { ok: true; fileId: string }
271	  | { ok: false; error: string }
272	
273	export async function attachFile(
274	  userId: string,
275	  versionId: string,
276	  r2Key: string,
277	  size: number,
278	  checksumSha256: string,
279	): Promise<AttachFileResult> {
280	  const fileId = crypto.randomUUID()
281	  const db = useDb()
282	
283	  const [owner] = await db
284	    .select({ publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId })
285	    .from(extensionVersions)
286	    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, extensionVersions.extensionId))
287	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
288	    .limit(1)
289	  if (!owner || owner.publisherUserId !== userId) {
290	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" }
291	  }
292	
293	  try {
294	    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
295	      await tx.insert(files).values({
296	        id: fileId,
297	        r2Key,
298	        size: BigInt(size),
299	        checksumSha256,
300	        mimeType: "application/zip",
301	        scanStatus: "pending",
302	      })
303	      const updated = await tx
304	        .update(extensionVersions)
305	        .set({ bundleFileId: fileId })
306	        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
307	        .returning({ id: extensionVersions.id })
308	      if (updated.length === 0) throw new Error("version_disappeared")
309	    })
310	  } catch (err) {
311	    console.error("[publish] attachFile failed", err)
312	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error" }
313	  }
314	  return { ok: true, fileId }
315	}
316	
317	export type SubmitResult =
318	  | { ok: true }
319	  | { ok: false; error: string }
320	
321	export async function submitForReview(
322	  userId: string,
323	  versionId: string,
324	): Promise<SubmitResult> {
325	  const db = useDb()
326	  const [version] = await db
327	    .select({
328	      bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,
329	      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,
330	    })
331	    .from(extensionVersions)
332	    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, extensionVersions.extensionId))
333	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
334	    .limit(1)
335	  if (!version || version.publisherUserId !== userId) {
336	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" }
337	  }
338	  if (!version.bundleFileId) return { ok: false, error: "no_bundle" }
339	
340	  try {
341	    await submit(versionId)
342	  } catch (err) {
343	    if (err instanceof VersionStateError) {
344	      return { ok: false, error: "version_not_submittable" }
345	    }
346	    console.error("[publish] submitForReview failed", err)
347	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error" }
348	  }
349	
350	  try {
351	    const { inngest } = await import("~~/server/utils/inngest")
352	    await inngest.send({
353	      name: "extension/scan.requested",
354	      data: { versionId, fileId: version.bundleFileId },
355	    })
356	  } catch (err) {
357	    console.error("[publish] inngest.send failed", err)
358	    try {
359	      await db
360	        .update(extensionVersions)
361	        .set({ status: "pending" })
362	        .where(
363	          and(
364	            eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId),
365	            eq(extensionVersions.status, "scanning"),
366	          ),
367	        )
368	    } catch (rollbackErr) {
369	      console.error("[publish] rollback after inngest failure failed", rollbackErr)
370	    }
371	    return { ok: false, error: "scan_queue_unavailable" }
372	  }
373	  return { ok: true }
374	}
375	
376	export async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {
377	  const db = useDb()
378	  // Aliased join against `users` so we can pull the display name for whoever
379	  // revoked the extension (if anyone) without colliding with the publisher's
380	  // own user row downstream.
381	  const revoker = alias(users, "revoker")
382	  const rows = await db
383	    .select({
384	      id: extensions.id,
385	      slug: extensions.slug,
386	      name: extensions.name,
387	      category: extensions.category,
388	      visibility: extensions.visibility,
389	      subCat: extensions.subCat,
390	      officialTier: extensions.officialTier,
391	      revokedAt: extensions.revokedAt,
392	      revokedByUserId: extensions.revokedByUserId,
393	      revocationNote: extensions.revocationNote,
394	      revokedByName: revoker.name,
395	      revokedByEmail: revoker.email,
396	      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,
397	      latestVersionId: extensionVersions.id,
398	      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,
399	      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,
400	      latestBundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,
401	    })
402	    .from(extensions)
403	    .leftJoin(
404	      extensionVersions,
405	      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),
406	    )
407	    .leftJoin(revoker, eq(revoker.id, extensions.revokedByUserId))
408	    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))
409	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.createdAt), desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))
410	
411	  const byExtension = new Map<string, (typeof rows)[number]>()
412	  for (const row of rows) {
413	    if (!byExtension.has(row.id)) byExtension.set(row.id, row)
414	  }
415	  return Array.from(byExtension.values())
416	}
417	
418	export async function discardDraft(
419	  userId: string,
420	  extensionId: string,
421	): Promise<{ ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }> {
422	  const db = useDb()
423	  const [row] = await db
424	    .select({
425	      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,
426	      visibility: extensions.visibility,
427	    })
428	    .from(extensions)
429	    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))
430	    .limit(1)
431	
432	  if (!row || row.publisherUserId !== userId) {
433	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" }
434	  }
435	  if (row.visibility !== "draft") {
436	    return { ok: false, error: "not_discardable" }
437	  }
438	
439	  await db.delete(extensions).where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))
440	  return { ok: true }
441	}
442

1	import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync, rmSync, statSync } from "fs";
2	import { writeFile } from "fs/promises";
3	import { homedir } from "os";
4	import { dirname, join } from "path";
5	import { unzipSync } from "fflate";
6	
7	const SKILLS_DIR = join(homedir(), ".claude", "skills");
8	
9	export async function installSkill(slug: string, zipBuffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
10	  const destDir = join(SKILLS_DIR, slug);
11	  if (!existsSync(SKILLS_DIR)) mkdirSync(SKILLS_DIR, { recursive: true });
12	  if (existsSync(destDir)) rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
13	  mkdirSync(destDir, { recursive: true });
14	
15	  const files = unzipSync(new Uint8Array(zipBuffer));
16	  try {
17	    for (const [entryPath, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
18	      if (entryPath.endsWith("/")) continue; // skip directory entries
19	      const dest = join(destDir, entryPath);
20	      const parentDir = dirname(dest);
21	      if (!existsSync(parentDir)) mkdirSync(parentDir, { recursive: true });
22	      await writeFile(dest, content);
23	    }
24	  } catch (err) {
25	    // Don't leave half-written skills on disk; the next install attempt will then
26	    // start from a clean slate instead of merging onto a corrupt tree.
27	    rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
28	    throw err;
29	  }
30	
31	  return destDir;
32	}
33	
34	export async function uninstallSkill(slug: string): Promise<boolean> {
35	  const destDir = join(SKILLS_DIR, slug);
36	  if (!existsSync(destDir)) return false;
37	  rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
38	  return true;
39	}
40	
41	export function listInstalledSlugs(): string[] {
42	  if (!existsSync(SKILLS_DIR)) return [];
43	  return readdirSync(SKILLS_DIR).filter((name: string) =>
44	    statSync(join(SKILLS_DIR, name)).isDirectory()
45	  );
46	}
47

40	
41	### `[compatibility]` — optional
42	
43	| Field | Type | Description |
44	|---|---|---|
45	| `minAgentVersion` | string | Minimum agent version required (semver, e.g. `0.8.0`). |
46	| `os` | string[] | Restrict to OS list: `darwin`, `linux`, `windows`. Omit = all. |
47	
48	### `[install.<agent>]` — at least one required
49	
50	Declares per-agent install destinations. The CLI reads the profile that matches
51	`agentcenter config get agent` (default: `claude`).
52	
53	**Skills** (`category = "skills"`):
54	
55	```toml
56	[install.claude]
57	skills = "~/.claude/skills/{slug}"
58	```
59	
60	**MCP Servers** (`category = "mcp"`):
61	
62	```toml
63	[install.claude]
64	mcpConfig = "~/.claude/claude_desktop_config.json"
65	mcpKey    = "{slug}"
66	```
67	
68	**Slash Commands** (`category = "slash"`):
69	
70	```toml
71	[install.claude]
72	commands = "~/.claude/commands/{slug}.md"
73	```
74	
75	**Plugins** (`category = "plugins"`):
76	
77	```toml
78	[install.claude]
79	plugins = "~/.claude/plugins/{slug}"
80	```
81	
82	Path tokens:
83	
84	| Token | Expands to |
85	|---|---|
86	| `{slug}` | Extension slug |
87	| `{version}` | Installed version |
88	| `{agent}` | Active agent profile name |
89	
90	Optional `[install.<agent>.postInstall]`:
91	
92	```toml

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/i-am-thinking-of-merry-locket.md",
  "content": "# Add `cli` as a new extension type\n\n## Context\n\nAgentCenter currently ships four extension categories — `skills`, `mcp`, `slash`, `plugins` — codified by the `extension_category` Postgres enum. We want a fifth: `cli`, a publisher-uploaded extension whose payload is an executable or installable package, parallel in every way to the existing four but installed to a different location on the user's machine. The existing surface area is fortunately well-factored for this: the manifest spec is already `[install.<agent>]`-keyed per category, the approvals matrix is keyed by `extensionCategory`, and the i18n labels live in two flat dictionaries. The bulk of the work is a mechanical parallel addition — the only design decision is the install destination, which we pin to `~/.claude/cli/{slug}` to mirror skills/plugins (directory drop, same uninstall semantics, user can symlink to PATH themselves).\n\nThis is additive to the frozen `/api/v1` contract — the `category` field gains one new accepted value. Existing CLI clients on older versions will see `cli` in API responses; they'll either skip unknown values or fail validation. The CLI must be released in lockstep so users can actually install `cli`-type extensions, but the schema change itself is non-breaking per the `/api/v1` rules.\n\n## Approach\n\nFive-part change, landed as one feature branch with conventional-commit phase scope `feat(p?-cli-type)`:\n\n1. **Enum + validator fanout** — every single source of truth for the four-value enum gets a fifth value\n2. **Manifest spec + bundle layer** — add a `[install.<agent>]` example for `cli`\n3. **CLI installer** — new `cli/src/installers/cli.ts` mirroring `skills.ts`; install/uninstall branches in `installers/index.ts`\n4. **Seed data** — approval-admin seed rows so the new tab in `/admin/approvals` isn't empty; optional demo extension rows so the listing page has content\n5. **UI surfaces** — i18n labels (en/zh), the legacy-category redirect map, and a `/cli` listing route mirroring the four existing ones\n\nDB migration: adding a value to a pg enum is a single `ALTER TYPE … ADD VALUE`. Drizzle Kit generates this from the schema diff — no manual SQL.\n\n## Critical files to modify\n\n### Enum / type single sources of truth (touch every one of these — drift will compile-error)\n\n- `shared/db/schema/extension.ts:29-34` — append `\"cli\"` to `extensionCategoryEnum`. Triggers a Drizzle migration via `bun run db:generate`.\n- `shared/types.ts:5` — append `\"cli\"` to `ExtensionCategory` union.\n- `shared/validators/manifest.ts:23` — append `\"cli\"` to the `z.enum` in `ExtensionManifestCore.category`.\n- `shared/validators/filters.ts:3` — append `\"cli\"` to `CATEGORIES`.\n- `shared/validators/approvals.ts:25` — append `\"cli\"` to the `ExtensionCategory` z.enum (this is a second copy of the same idea, used by the approvals layer; both must stay in sync — flag for future consolidation but not in this PR).\n- `shared/data/approval-admins.ts:22` — append `\"cli\"` to `SeedExtensionCategory`.\n\n### Category-branched logic (extend the switch with a `cli` case)\n\n- `server/utils/publish.ts:22-34` — `defaultClassification()`: add `case \"cli\": return { funcCat: \"tools\", subCat: \"cli\" }`. Pick `tools/cli` as the natural taxonomy slot.\n- `cli/src/installers/index.ts:8-25` — both `installExtension` and `uninstallExtension` switches: add `case \"cli\": return installCli(...) / uninstallCli(...)`.\n- `app/middleware/legacy-categories.global.ts:1-7` — append `cli: \"/cli\"` to `CATEGORY_REDIRECTS`.\n\n### i18n labels (two files, mirror keys)\n\n- `i18n/locales/en.json` and `i18n/locales/zh.json` — every place that enumerates the four categories needs a `cli` key. From the grep:\n  - `nav.search.{cat}.label` + `nav.search.{cat}.searchPlaceholder` (around line 110–124 in en.json)\n  - `nav.{skills,mcpServers,slashCommands,plugins}` top-level labels (around line 145–148) — add a `cli` sibling\n  - `publish.options.category.*` (around line 336–340)\n  - The admin approvals matrix labels (around line 587–590)\n  - Any home/landing copy that lists category counts (search for `discoveryHeading` neighbours)\n  - Suggested labels: en `\"CLI\"`, zh `\"命令行工具\"` — confirm zh wording with user if uncertain.\n\n### Manifest spec docs\n\n- `docs/manifest-spec.md:48-95` — add a fifth subsection between Plugins and Path-tokens:\n  ```toml\n  **CLI tools** (`category = \"cli\"`):\n\n  [install.claude]\n  cli = \"~/.claude/cli/{slug}\"\n  ```\n  Note that the directory may contain an executable, a package, or both — the CLI does a directory drop and the publisher's `postInstall.message` can guide users on PATH wiring or `npm link`.\n\n### Approval-admin seed rows\n\n- `shared/data/approval-admins.ts:36-91` — at minimum:\n  - One company-tier `all/*` row (mirror line 41–44 pattern, `reviewerEmail: \"amy@agentcenter.dev\"`) so super-admin coverage extends to the new tab.\n  - Two macro rows so the new tab isn't visibly empty when an admin opens it — e.g., `cli × company × macro × softDev → ben` and `cli × company × macro × cloud → cory`.\n  - One PL `all/*` row (e.g., wireless → dao) so PL queues route.\n  - No need to seed a pending request unless you also want the queue to light up on `bun run db:seed`.\n\n### Optional demo extensions\n\n- `shared/data/extensions.ts` — add 1–2 entries with `category: \"cli\"` so the new `/cli` listing has content on a fresh DB. Mirror the shape of the existing `category: \"plugins\"` entries (lines 72, 168, 303). Skip if you'd rather the seed list stay lean.\n\n## New files to create\n\n### CLI installer\n\n- `cli/src/installers/cli.ts` — mirror `cli/src/installers/skills.ts` exactly; only difference is `const CLI_DIR = join(homedir(), \".claude\", \"cli\")`. The directory-drop + `unzipSync` + per-entry write pattern is identical, including the rollback-on-error and `listInstalledSlugs` helper.\n\n### Listing page\n\n- `app/pages/cli.vue` — mirror the existing `skills.vue` / `mcp.vue` / `commands.vue` / `plugins.vue` listing pages. These wrap the same `ExtensionListing` component with a fixed `category` prop. (If the existing four are colocated under a shared component, just register the new route there — Explore agent flagged `ExtensionListing.vue:~32` but didn't enumerate page files; verify which pattern is in use before writing this file.) The MCP panorama link branch in `ExtensionListing.vue:~32` is mcp-specific; no `cli` branch needed.\n\n## Migration\n\n`bun run db:generate` after editing `shared/db/schema/extension.ts` produces a migration like:\n\n```sql\nALTER TYPE \"extension_category\" ADD VALUE 'cli';\n```\n\n`ALTER TYPE ... ADD VALUE` cannot run inside a transaction in Postgres < 12 but is fine on Supabase's Postgres 15+; Drizzle Kit handles this correctly. Run `bun run db:migrate` to apply locally.\n\n## Reused functions / utilities (don't reinvent)\n\n- **Install primitive**: `installSkill` at `cli/src/installers/skills.ts:9` — copy its body verbatim into `installCli` with the dest dir changed. Same rollback semantics, same `unzipSync`, same dir-entry skip.\n- **Category z.enum in `ExtensionManifestCore`** at `shared/validators/manifest.ts:14` is `.pick()`'d by `BundleManifestSchema` at line 84 — extending the parent automatically extends the child; no bundle-schema edit needed.\n- **Default classification fallback**: `defaultClassification()` at `server/utils/publish.ts:22` already has a `default` branch returning `tools/general`; if you'd rather not pick a `cli`-specific subCat, removing the new case lets it fall through to that default. Choose explicitly for taxonomy filter UX.\n\n## Open question to confirm before commit\n\n- **subCat for `cli`**: I've proposed `tools/cli` in the default classification. If you prefer it fall through to `tools/general` (matching the current skills/plugins default), say so. This affects the FUNC_TAXONOMY filter rail behaviour only — not the listing route.\n\n## Commit split (per CLAUDE.md §13)\n\nThree commits on one branch, in order:\n\n1. `feat(p?-cli-type)!: add cli extension category` — schema enum + types + validators + migration. The `!` is conservative: technically additive on `/api/v1`, but downstream CLIs that hard-validate the enum will reject `cli` rows until upgraded. Confirm with user whether `!` is warranted.\n2. `feat(p?-cli-type): wire cli installer and manifest spec` — `cli/src/installers/cli.ts`, the installers/index switch, and `docs/manifest-spec.md`.\n3. `feat(p?-cli-type): seed and surface cli category in UI` — approval-admin seed rows, i18n labels (en+zh), `legacy-categories.global.ts`, `app/pages/cli.vue`, optional demo extensions.\n\n## Verification\n\nEnd-to-end on a clean checkout:\n\n1. `bun run db:generate && bun run db:migrate` — migration applies cleanly, no diff drift.\n2. `SEED_PASSWORD=test bun run db:seed` — seed succeeds; the four existing approval-admin rows plus the new `cli` rows all insert.\n3. `bun run dev` and visit `/cli` — listing renders with the i18n label, optional demo extensions visible if seeded.\n4. Sign in as amy (super-admin) and visit `/admin/approvals` — the matrix shows a `CLI` tab with the seeded admin rows visible.\n5. Run the publish wizard end-to-end with `category: \"cli\"`, upload a small zip, submit for review. Confirm the draft → scanning → ready state transition works (no category-specific branches in `extensions-state.ts` to break).\n6. From a separate terminal: `cd cli && bun run build && ./dist/bin/agentcenter install <slug>` against the local dev server. Confirm files land in `~/.claude/cli/<slug>/` and `agentcenter list` shows the entry. Then `agentcenter uninstall <slug>` and confirm the directory is gone.\n7. `bun run validate` (typecheck + lint + unit + e2e per docs/plan.md §13) passes — coverage thresholds hold; the new enum value should not regress existing tests, and the few that hard-code the four-value list (`shared/validators/manifest.test.ts:18`, `shared/search/query.test.ts:22`, `shared/validators/approvals.test.ts:186+`) keep working because they assert with `skills`, not with `.length === 4`. Add at least one test row covering `category: \"cli\"` to `filters.test.ts` and `manifest.test.ts` to lock the contract.\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Add `cli` as a new extension type

## Context

AgentCenter currently ships four extension categories — `skills`, `mcp`, `slash`, `plugins` — codified by the `extension_category` Postgres enum. We want a fifth: `cli`, a publisher-uploaded extension whose payload is an executable or installable package, parallel in every way to the existing four but installed to a different location on the user's machine. The existing surface area is fortunately well-factored for this: the manifest spec is already `[install.<agent>]`-keyed per category, the approvals matrix is keyed by `extensionCategory`, and the i18n labels live in two flat dictionaries. The bulk of the work is a mechanical parallel addition — the only design decision is the install destination, which we pin to `~/.claude/cli/{slug}` to mirror skills/plugins (directory drop, same uninstall semantics, user can symlink to PATH themselves).

This is additive to the frozen `/api/v1` contract — the `category` field gains one new accepted value. Existing CLI clients on older versions will see `cli` in API responses; they'll either skip unknown values or fail validation. The CLI must be released in lockstep so users can actually install `cli`-type extensions, but the schema change itself is non-breaking per the `/api/v1` rules.

## Approach

Five-part change, landed as one feature branch with conventional-commit phase scope `feat(p?-cli-type)`:

1. **Enum + validator fanout** — every single source of truth for the four-value enum gets a fifth value
2. **Manifest spec + bundle layer** — add a `[install.<agent>]` example for `cli`
3. **CLI installer** — new `cli/src/installers/cli.ts` mirroring `skills.ts`; install/uninstall branches in `installers/index.ts`
4. **Seed data** — approval-admin seed rows so the new tab in `/admin/approvals` isn't empty; optional demo extension rows so the listing page has content
5. **UI surfaces** — i18n labels (en/zh), the legacy-category redirect map, and a `/cli` listing route mirroring the four existing ones

DB migration: adding a value to a pg enum is a single `ALTER TYPE … ADD VALUE`. Drizzle Kit generates this from the schema diff — no manual SQL.

## Critical files to modify

### Enum / type single sources of truth (touch every one of these — drift will compile-error)

- `shared/db/schema/extension.ts:29-34` — append `"cli"` to `extensionCategoryEnum`. Triggers a Drizzle migration via `bun run db:generate`.
- `shared/types.ts:5` — append `"cli"` to `ExtensionCategory` union.
- `shared/validators/manifest.ts:23` — append `"cli"` to the `z.enum` in `ExtensionManifestCore.category`.
- `shared/validators/filters.ts:3` — append `"cli"` to `CATEGORIES`.
- `shared/validators/approvals.ts:25` — append `"cli"` to the `ExtensionCategory` z.enum (this is a second copy of the same idea, used by the approvals layer; both must stay in sync — flag for future consolidation but not in this PR).
- `shared/data/approval-admins.ts:22` — append `"cli"` to `SeedExtensionCategory`.

### Category-branched logic (extend the switch with a `cli` case)

- `server/utils/publish.ts:22-34` — `defaultClassification()`: add `case "cli": return { funcCat: "tools", subCat: "cli" }`. Pick `tools/cli` as the natural taxonomy slot.
- `cli/src/installers/index.ts:8-25` — both `installExtension` and `uninstallExtension` switches: add `case "cli": return installCli(...) / uninstallCli(...)`.
- `app/middleware/legacy-categories.global.ts:1-7` — append `cli: "/cli"` to `CATEGORY_REDIRECTS`.

### i18n labels (two files, mirror keys)

- `i18n/locales/en.json` and `i18n/locales/zh.json` — every place that enumerates the four categories needs a `cli` key. From the grep:
  - `nav.search.{cat}.label` + `nav.search.{cat}.searchPlaceholder` (around line 110–124 in en.json)
  - `nav.{skills,mcpServers,slashCommands,plugins}` top-level labels (around line 145–148) — add a `cli` sibling
  - `publish.options.category.*` (around line 336–340)
  - The admin approvals matrix labels (around line 587–590)
  - Any home/landing copy that lists category counts (search for `discoveryHeading` neighbours)
  - Suggested labels: en `"CLI"`, zh `"命令行工具"` — confirm zh wording with user if uncertain.

### Manifest spec docs

- `docs/manifest-spec.md:48-95` — add a fifth subsection between Plugins and Path-tokens:
  ```toml
  **CLI tools** (`category = "cli"`):

  [install.claude]
  cli = "~/.claude/cli/{slug}"
  ```
  Note that the directory may contain an executable, a package, or both — the CLI does a directory drop and the publisher's `postInstall.message` can guide users on PATH wiring or `npm link`.

### Approval-admin seed rows

- `shared/data/approval-admins.ts:36-91` — at minimum:
  - One company-tier `all/*` row (mirror line 41–44 pattern, `reviewerEmail: "amy@agentcenter.dev"`) so super-admin coverage extends to the new tab.
  - Two macro rows so the new tab isn't visibly empty when an admin opens it — e.g., `cli × company × macro × softDev → ben` and `cli × company × macro × cloud → cory`.
  - One PL `all/*` row (e.g., wireless → dao) so PL queues route.
  - No need to seed a pending request unless you also want the queue to light up on `bun run db:seed`.

### Optional demo extensions

- `shared/data/extensions.ts` — add 1–2 entries with `category: "cli"` so the new `/cli` listing has content on a fresh DB. Mirror the shape of the existing `category: "plugins"` entries (lines 72, 168, 303). Skip if you'd rather the seed list stay lean.

## New files to create

### CLI installer

- `cli/src/installers/cli.ts` — mirror `cli/src/installers/skills.ts` exactly; only difference is `const CLI_DIR = join(homedir(), ".claude", "cli")`. The directory-drop + `unzipSync` + per-entry write pattern is identical, including the rollback-on-error and `listInstalledSlugs` helper.

### Listing page

- `app/pages/cli.vue` — mirror the existing `skills.vue` / `mcp.vue` / `commands.vue` / `plugins.vue` listing pages. These wrap the same `ExtensionListing` component with a fixed `category` prop. (If the existing four are colocated under a shared component, just register the new route there — Explore agent flagged `ExtensionListing.vue:~32` but didn't enumerate page files; verify which pattern is in use before writing this file.) The MCP panorama link branch in `ExtensionListing.vue:~32` is mcp-specific; no `cli` branch needed.

## Migration

`bun run db:generate` after editing `shared/db/schema/extension.ts` produces a migration like:

```sql
ALTER TYPE "extension_category" ADD VALUE 'cli';
```

`ALTER TYPE ... ADD VALUE` cannot run inside a transaction in Postgres < 12 but is fine on Supabase's Postgres 15+; Drizzle Kit handles this correctly. Run `bun run db:migrate` to apply locally.

## Reused functions / utilities (don't reinvent)

- **Install primitive**: `installSkill` at `cli/src/installers/skills.ts:9` — copy its body verbatim into `installCli` with the dest dir changed. Same rollback semantics, same `unzipSync`, same dir-entry skip.
- **Category z.enum in `ExtensionManifestCore`** at `shared/validators/manifest.ts:14` is `.pick()`'d by `BundleManifestSchema` at line 84 — extending the parent automatically extends the child; no bundle-schema edit needed.
- **Default classification fallback**: `defaultClassification()` at `server/utils/publish.ts:22` already has a `default` branch returning `tools/general`; if you'd rather not pick a `cli`-specific subCat, removing the new case lets it fall through to that default. Choose explicitly for taxonomy filter UX.

## Open question to confirm before commit

- **subCat for `cli`**: I've proposed `tools/cli` in the default classification. If you prefer it fall through to `tools/general` (matching the current skills/plugins default), say so. This affects the FUNC_TAXONOMY filter rail behaviour only — not the listing route.

## Branch, commit split, PR (per CLAUDE.md §13)

1. **Branch**: `git checkout -b feat/cli-extension-type` off the current `main`.
2. **Commits** (three, in order, each runs `bun run validate` locally before pushing):
   1. `feat(p?-cli-type)!: add cli extension category` — schema enum + types + validators + migration. The `!` is conservative: technically additive on `/api/v1`, but downstream CLIs that hard-validate the enum will reject `cli` rows until upgraded. I'll drop the `!` if you prefer, since the contract widening is forward-compatible for clients that ignore unknown values.
   2. `feat(p?-cli-type): wire cli installer and manifest spec` — `cli/src/installers/cli.ts`, the installers/index switch, and `docs/manifest-spec.md`.
   3. `feat(p?-cli-type): seed and surface cli category in UI` — approval-admin seed rows, i18n labels (en+zh), `legacy-categories.global.ts`, `app/pages/cli.vue`, optional demo extensions.
3. **PR**: `gh pr create` against `main`. PR body summarises the additive contract change, lists the touched single-source-of-truth files, and links the manifest-spec update. No `Co-Authored-By` trailer and no Claude-generated footer (per CLAUDE.md).
4. **Push and stop**: do not merge; wait for human review per the phase-boundary checkpoint rule.

## Verification

End-to-end on a clean checkout:

1. `bun run db:generate && bun run db:migrate` — migration applies cleanly, no diff drift.
2. `SEED_PASSWORD=test bun run db:seed` — seed succeeds; the four existing approval-admin rows plus the new `cli` rows all insert.
3. `bun run dev` and visit `/cli` — listing renders with the i18n label, optional demo extensions visible if seeded.
4. Sign in as amy (super-admin) and visit `/admin/approvals` — the matrix shows a `CLI` tab with the seeded admin rows visible.
5. Run the publish wizard end-to-end with `category: "cli"`, upload a small zip, submit for review. Confirm the draft → scanning → ready state transition works (no category-specific branches in `extensions-state.ts` to break).
6. From a separate terminal: `cd cli && bun run build && ./dist/bin/agentcenter install <slug>` against the local dev server. Confirm files land in `~/.claude/cli/<slug>/` and `agentcenter list` shows the entry. Then `agentcenter uninstall <slug>` and confirm the directory is gone.
7. `bun run validate` (typecheck + lint + unit + e2e per docs/plan.md §13) passes — coverage thresholds hold; the new enum value should not regress existing tests, and the few that hard-code the four-value list (`shared/validators/manifest.test.ts:18`, `shared/search/query.test.ts:22`, `shared/validators/approvals.test.ts:186+`) keep working because they assert with `skills`, not with `.length === 4`. Add at least one test row covering `category: "cli"` to `filters.test.ts` and `manifest.test.ts` to lock the contract.

```

3	import {
4	  ALL_L1_KEYS,
5	  ALL_L2_KEYS,
6	  type CategoryLevel,
7	} from "~~/shared/taxonomy"
8	
9	const L1_KEY_SET = new Set<string>(ALL_L1_KEYS)
10	const L2_KEY_SET = new Set<string>(ALL_L2_KEYS)
11	
12	// `SUB_CAT_KEY_LIST` is the source of truth for the publisher subCat picker
13	// and the request-row validator — same set as `ALL_L1_KEYS`, just re-exported
14	// under the historical name so the matrix UI and the publish dialog stay
15	// in lockstep.
16	export const SUB_CAT_KEY_LIST: readonly string[] = ALL_L1_KEYS
17	
18	export const OfficialTier = z.enum(["productLine", "company"])
19	export type OfficialTier = z.infer<typeof OfficialTier>
20	
21	export const SubCatKey = z.string().refine((v) => L1_KEY_SET.has(v), {
22	  message: "must be a key from FUNC_TAXONOMY",
23	})
24	
25	export const ExtensionCategory = z.enum(["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"])
26	export type ExtensionCategory = z.infer<typeof ExtensionCategory>
27	
28	export const AdminCategoryLevel = z.enum(["all", "macro", "micro"])
29	export type AdminCategoryLevel = CategoryLevel
30	
31	// Product-line ids are seeded text PKs in kebab-case (wireless, datacom,
32	// terminals, cloud as of 0009). Validate the shape here; the DB FK + CHECK
33	// constraint enforces membership and the iff-rule against the actual tier.
34	export const ProductLineId = z
35	  .string()
36	  .trim()
37	  .min(1)
38	  .max(40)
39	  .regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/, {
40	    message: "must be a kebab-case product-line id",
41	  })
42	export type ProductLineId = z.infer<typeof ProductLineId>
43	
44	// iff-rule helpers — the DB CHECK is the source of truth, but surfacing
45	// the rule at the validator layer turns a bad request into a typed Zod
46	// error instead of a generic 23514 from Postgres.
47	const requireProductLineIff = <T extends {
48	  tier?: OfficialTier
49	  requestedTier?: OfficialTier
50	  productLineId?: string | undefined
51	}>(
52	  v: T,
53	  tierKey: "tier" | "requestedTier",
54	): boolean => {
55	  const tier = v[tierKey]
56	  const hasPl = !!v.productLineId
57	  if (tier === "productLine") return hasPl
58	  if (tier === "company") return !hasPl
59	  return true
60	}

1	import * as extensionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/extensions"
2	import { listAllProductLines } from "~~/server/repositories/productLines"
3	import * as versionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/versions"
4	
5	// Detail endpoint adds a resolved `productLineLabel` so the hero badge can
6	// render "Wireless Official" / "无线官方" without a second round-trip. The
7	// page passes its i18n locale as a `?locale=` query param — locked
8	// decision #5 makes locales always-prefixed in URLs, so the page already
9	// knows the canonical locale and we don't need an accept-language parser
10	// here. Unknown / missing locale falls back to English.
11	export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
12	  const query = getQuery(event)
13	  const slug = typeof query.slug === "string" ? query.slug : null
14	  if (!slug) throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: "slug required" })
15	  const locale = typeof query.locale === "string" ? query.locale : "en"
16	
17	  const db = useDb()
18	  const ext = await extensionsRepo.findBySlug(db, slug)
19	  if (!ext) throw createError({ statusCode: 404, statusMessage: "not_found" })
20	
21	  const [versions, related, productLines] = await Promise.all([
22	    versionsRepo.listForExtension(db, ext.id),
23	    extensionsRepo.findRelated(db, ext.id, ext.category),
24	    ext.productLineId ? listAllProductLines(db) : Promise.resolve([]),
25	  ])
26	
27	  const productLine = ext.productLineId
28	    ? productLines.find((l) => l.id === ext.productLineId)
29	    : null
30	  const productLineLabel = productLine
31	    ? locale === "zh"
32	      ? productLine.labelZh
33	      : productLine.labelEn
34	    : null
35	
36	  return { ext: { ...ext, productLineLabel }, versions, related }
37	})
38

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/cli/src/installers/cli.ts",
  "content": "import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync, rmSync, statSync } from \"fs\";\nimport { writeFile } from \"fs/promises\";\nimport { homedir } from \"os\";\nimport { dirname, join } from \"path\";\nimport { unzipSync } from \"fflate\";\n\nconst CLI_DIR = join(homedir(), \".claude\", \"cli\");\n\nexport async function installCli(slug: string, zipBuffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {\n  const destDir = join(CLI_DIR, slug);\n  if (!existsSync(CLI_DIR)) mkdirSync(CLI_DIR, { recursive: true });\n  if (existsSync(destDir)) rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });\n  mkdirSync(destDir, { recursive: true });\n\n  const files = unzipSync(new Uint8Array(zipBuffer));\n  try {\n    for (const [entryPath, content] of Object.entries(files)) {\n      if (entryPath.endsWith(\"/\")) continue; // skip directory entries\n      const dest = join(destDir, entryPath);\n      const parentDir = dirname(dest);\n      if (!existsSync(parentDir)) mkdirSync(parentDir, { recursive: true });\n      await writeFile(dest, content);\n    }\n  } catch (err) {\n    rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });\n    throw err;\n  }\n\n  return destDir;\n}\n\nexport async function uninstallCli(slug: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  const destDir = join(CLI_DIR, slug);\n  if (!existsSync(destDir)) return false;\n  rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });\n  return true;\n}\n\nexport function listInstalledCliSlugs(): string[] {\n  if (!existsSync(CLI_DIR)) return [];\n  return readdirSync(CLI_DIR).filter((name: string) =>\n    statSync(join(CLI_DIR, name)).isDirectory()\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "import { installSkill, uninstallSkill, listInstalledSlugs } from \"./skills\";\n\nexport async function installExtension(\n  slug: string,\n  category: string,\n  zipBuffer: ArrayBuffer,\n): Promise<string> {\n  switch (category) {\n    case \"skill\":\n    case \"skills\":\n      return installSkill(slug, zipBuffer);\n    default:\n      // Fallback: treat everything else as a skill-style directory drop\n      return installSkill(slug, zipBuffer);\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function uninstallExtension(slug: string, category: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  switch (category) {\n    case \"skill\":\n    case \"skills\":\n      return uninstallSkill(slug);\n    default:\n      return uninstallSkill(slug);\n  }\n}\n\nexport { listInstalledSlugs };",
  "new_string": "import { installSkill, uninstallSkill, listInstalledSlugs } from \"./skills\";\nimport { installCli, uninstallCli } from \"./cli\";\n\nexport async function installExtension(\n  slug: string,\n  category: string,\n  zipBuffer: ArrayBuffer,\n): Promise<string> {\n  switch (category) {\n    case \"skill\":\n    case \"skills\":\n      return installSkill(slug, zipBuffer);\n    case \"cli\":\n      return installCli(slug, zipBuffer);\n    default:\n      // Fallback: treat everything else as a skill-style directory drop\n      return installSkill(slug, zipBuffer);\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function uninstallExtension(slug: string, category: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  switch (category) {\n    case \"skill\":\n    case \"skills\":\n      return uninstallSkill(slug);\n    case \"cli\":\n      return uninstallCli(slug);\n    default:\n      return uninstallSkill(slug);\n  }\n}\n\nexport { listInstalledSlugs };"
}

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --base main --title \"feat(p10-cli-type): add CLI as a new extension type\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nAdds `cli` as a fifth `extension_category` value alongside `skills` / `mcp` / `slash` / `plugins`. CLI extensions are publisher-uploaded bundles that the Agent CLI installs to `~/.claude/cli/{slug}` — a directory drop with the same rollback semantics as `skills`. The contents may be an executable, an installable package (npm/uv), or both; PATH wiring or package-manager invocation is handled by the publisher's `[install.<agent>.postInstall].message`, not the installer.\n\nThe change is additive to the frozen `/api/v1` contract — older CLI clients see an unknown `category` value but no existing field shape changes.\n\n## Commits\n\n1. **`feat(p10-cli-type): add cli extension category to schema and validators`** — schema enum + Drizzle ALTER TYPE migration (`0013_nostalgic_slapstick.sql`) + every type-level single source of truth widened (`shared/types.ts`, `shared/validators/manifest.ts`, `shared/validators/filters.ts`, `shared/validators/approvals.ts`, `shared/data/approval-admins.ts`) + the five local enum duplicates flushed out by typecheck (`server/repositories/extensions.ts:findRelated`, `app/components/extension/ExtensionListing.vue`, `app/components/approvals/ReviewerMatrix.vue` + its tab array, `app/pages/admin/approvals/index.vue`, `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` + its `hrefFor` / `isCategoryActive` / `MORE_ITEMS`).\n2. **`feat(p10-cli-type): wire cli installer and manifest spec`** — `cli/src/installers/cli.ts` mirroring `skills.ts` (target `~/.claude/cli/{slug}`), `installExtension`/`uninstallExtension` switches routed to it, and a `[install.claude] cli = \"~/.claude/cli/{slug}\"` example added to `docs/manifest-spec.md` with a note on the post-install hand-off.\n3. **`feat(p10-cli-type): seed and surface cli category in UI`** — `legacy-categories.global.ts` routes `?category=cli` → `/cli`; new `app/pages/cli/index.vue` mirrors `mcp/index.vue`; `publish.defaultClassification()` maps `cli` to `tools/cli`; approval-admin seed gets one company-tier All + two macros (softDev → ben, cloud → cory) + a PL wireless All so the new ext-type tab in `/admin/approvals` isn't visibly empty; i18n EN/ZH labels for `extensions.category.cli`, `sidebar.cli`, `publish.options.category.cli`, and the admin matrix `cat.cli`.\n\n## Notes\n\n- Tech-debt observation, not addressed here: the `ExtensionCategory` enum is duplicated in **eleven** places (one canonical type + one Drizzle pgEnum + four validator z.enums + five local-component duplicates). FILE: cli/src/installers/cli.ts LINE: 23
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🔴 Critical_ | _⚡ Quick win_

**Validate `slug` and ZIP entry paths before filesystem writes/deletes.**

Line 10 and Line 19 currently trust path segments, so `../` or absolute paths can escape `~/.claude/cli` (same risk for Line 33 deletes). This is a path traversal vulnerability.

   

<details>
<summary>Suggested fix</summary>

```diff
 import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync, rmSync, statSync } from "fs";
 import { writeFile } from "fs/promises";
 import { homedir } from "os";
-import { dirname, join } from "path";
+import { dirname, join, resolve, sep } from "path";
 import { unzipSync } from "fflate";

 const CLI_DIR = join(homedir(), ".claude", "cli");
+
+function resolveInside(baseDir: string, unsafePath: string): string {
+  const resolved = resolve(baseDir, unsafePath);
+  if (!(resolved === baseDir || resolved.startsWith(baseDir + sep))) {
+    throw new Error(`Path escapes install root: ${unsafePath}`);
+  }
+  return resolved;
+}
+
+function assertValidSlug(slug: string): void {
+  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/.test(slug)) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid CLI slug");
+  }
+}

 export async function installCli(slug: string, zipBuffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
-  const destDir = join(CLI_DIR, slug);
+  assertValidSlug(slug);
+  const destDir = resolveInside(CLI_DIR, slug);
   if (!existsSync(CLI_DIR)) mkdirSync(CLI_DIR, { recursive: true });
   if (existsSync(destDir)) rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   mkdirSync(destDir, { recursive: true });

   const files = unzipSync(new Uint8Array(zipBuffer));
   try {
     for (const [entryPath, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
       if (entryPath.endsWith("/")) continue; // skip directory entries
-      const dest = join(destDir, entryPath);
+      const dest = resolveInside(destDir, entryPath);
       const parentDir = dirname(dest);
       if (!existsSync(parentDir)) mkdirSync(parentDir, { recursive: true });
       await writeFile(dest, content);
     }
   } catch (err) {
@@
 export async function uninstallCli(slug: string): Promise<boolean> {
-  const destDir = join(CLI_DIR, slug);
+  assertValidSlug(slug);
+  const destDir = resolveInside(CLI_DIR, slug);
   if (!existsSync(destDir)) return false;
   rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   return true;
 }
```
</details>

9	export async function installSkill(slug: string, zipBuffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
10	  const destDir = join(SKILLS_DIR, slug);
11	  if (!existsSync(SKILLS_DIR)) mkdirSync(SKILLS_DIR, { recursive: true });
12	  if (existsSync(destDir)) rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
13	  mkdirSync(destDir, { recursive: true });
14	
15	  const files = unzipSync(new Uint8Array(zipBuffer));
16	  try {
17	    for (const [entryPath, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
18	      if (entryPath.endsWith("/")) continue; // skip directory entries
19	      const dest = join(destDir, entryPath);
20	      const parentDir = dirname(dest);
21	      if (!existsSync(parentDir)) mkdirSync(parentDir, { recursive: true });
22	      await writeFile(dest, content);
23	    }
24	  } catch (err) {
25	    // Don't leave half-written skills on disk; the next install attempt will then
26	    // start from a clean slate instead of merging onto a corrupt tree.
27	    rmSync(destDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
28	    throw err;
29	  }
30	
31	  return destDir;
32	}
